MEMORANDUM
OM-CFSSLG-2026-003
FOR:
VICE PRESIDENT
SECRETARY
TREASURER
AUDITOR
PUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICER
PROTOCOL OFFICER
ALL CLUSTER IV FEDERATION BOARD MEMBERSALL OTHER CONCERNED
FROM:
HON. WENDELL RENZ M. DIAMSAYPresident, Cluster IV Federation S.Y. 2026-2027
SUBJECT:Nationwide Online Survey on Digital Device Use in Schools
DATE:September 1, 2026
1. In line with OM-NFSSLG-2026-001 dated 25 August 2026, titled "Nationwide Online Survey on Digital Device Use in Schools," all concerned are enjoined to support, disseminate, and participate in the survey in accordance with the said Memorandum and its annexes.
2. All Regional Federation officers, representatives, and members are encouraged to accomplish the appropriate survey, where applicable, and facilitate its dissemination to the intended respondents within their respective areas of responsibility.
3. Participation is voluntary. No individual shall be compelled to participate, provide a particular response, or submit screenshots, copies, or individual responses as proof of participation.
4. All activities shall observe Republic Act No. 10173 (Data Privacy Act of 2012) and applicable data privacy and information security policies. Monitoring and reporting shall be limited to aggregate dissemination and participation data.
5. The appropriate official survey link shall be used for the designated respondents (See Annex B).
6. The survey shall be accomplished on or before 4 September 2026 (Friday), unless otherwise extended by the National Federation.
7. For inquiries or clarification, please coordinate with the Cluster Federation of Supreme Secondary Learner Government (CFSSLG) through hannahleahcorpuz636@gmail.com
8. For information, guidance, and appropriate action.

===== OM_SSLG_2026_001 (1).docx =====
MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHER CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, LGCC S.Y. 2026-2027President, SSLG S.Y. 2026-2027
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 25, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and
Policy Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. SOPHIA ELISE C. AGUAS, Public Information Officer/Spokesperson at sophiaagua0223@gmail.com or via Facebook (sophia.aguas.273184).
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee
DESIGNATION
NAME
Chairperson

Hon. Yuan B. Cutiongco
ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

===== OM_SSLG_2026_001 (11).docx =====
MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHERS CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 27, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and Policy
Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. AARON Y. HERNANDEZ – Vice President/Spokesperson at aaronher1030@gmail.com or www.facebook.com/aaron.hernandez.914981.
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHERS CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 27, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and Policy
Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. AARON Y. HERNANDEZ – Vice President/Spokesperson at aaronher1030@gmail.com or www.facebook.com/aaron.hernandez.914981.
For strict compliance.

MEMORANDUM
No. 002, s. 2026
TO:SECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHERS CONCERNED
FROM: By the Authority of the SSLG President
Hon. Aaron Y. HernandezVice President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
NATIONWIDE ONLINE SURVEY ON DIGITAL DEVICE USE IN SCHOOLS
DATE: AUGUST 27, 2026
1. In line with OM-NFSSLG-2026-001 dated 25 August 2026, titled “Nationwide Online Survey on Digital Device Use in Schools,” all concerned are enjoined to support, disseminate, and participate in the survey in accordance with the said Memorandum and its annexes.
2. All concerned are encouraged to accomplish the appropriate survey, where applicable, and facilitate its dissemination to the intended respondents within their respective areas of responsibility.
3. Participation is voluntary. No individual shall be compelled to participate, provide a particular response, or submit screenshots, copies, or individual responses as proof of participation.
4. All activities shall observe Republic Act No. 10173 (Data Privacy Act of 2012) and applicable data privacy and information security policies. Monitoring and reporting shall be limited to aggregate dissemination and participation data.
5. The appropriate official survey link shall be used for the designated respondents (See Annex B).
6. The survey shall be accomplished on or before 4 September 2026 (Friday), unless otherwise extended by the National Federation.
7.1. For inquiries or clarifications, please contact Hon. AARON Y. HERNANDEZ – Vice President/Spokesperson at aaronher1030@gmail.com or www.facebook.com/aaron.hernandez.914981.
8. For information, guidance, and appropriate action.

MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHERS CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 27, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and Policy
Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. AARON Y. HERNANDEZ – Vice President/Spokesperson at aaronher1030@gmail.com or www.facebook.com/aaron.hernandez.914981.
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that events are visually organized and aligned with the theme.
Partner with Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine suitable actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.
Prepared by:
RHIAN JADE L. CASTROSSLG President
Reviewed and Evaluated by:
AARON KHRISTIAN T. BANAGSSLG Adviser
Concurred:
AMPARO M. MUÑOZ, EDDPrincipal IV

GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.

Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.

Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.

Manage official social media and information dissemination.

Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.

Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHER CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 25, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and
Policy Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. SOPHIA ELISE C. AGUAS, Public Information Officer/Spokesperson at sophiaagua0223@gmail.com or via Facebook (sophia.aguas.273184).
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.

F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

===== OM_SSLG_2026_001 (7).docx =====
MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHER CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 25, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and
Policy Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. SOPHIA ELISE C. AGUAS, Public Information Officer/Spokesperson at sophiaagua0223@gmail.com or via Facebook (sophia.aguas.273184).
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that events are visually organized and aligned with the theme.
Partner with Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine suitable actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

RHIAN JADE L. CASTROSSLG President
Reviewed and Evaluated by:
AARON KHRISTIAN T. BANAGSSLG Adviser

MEMORANDUM
No. 001, s. 2026
FOR:VICE PRESIDENTSECRETARYTREASURERAUDITORPUBLIC INFORMATION OFFICERPROTOCOL OFFICERGRADE-LEVEL REPRESENTATIVESALL OTHER CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President
Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
MANDATORY ESTABLISHMENT AND IMPLEMENTATION OF STANDING COMMITTEES IN THE SCHOOL SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG), WITH GUIDING FRAMEWORK FOR LGCC IMPLEMENTATION, S.Y. 2026–2027
DATE: May 25, 2026
Pursuant to Division Memorandum OM-DFSSLG-2026-002 and in alignment with the directives of the Division and Cluster Federations, this Office hereby directs the establishment and implementation of Standing Committees within the School Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG). All concerned are expected to comply accordingly.
This initiative is undertaken to establish a structured, accountable, and efficient system of governance within the SSLG through functional delegation of responsibilities. This issuance shall likewise serve as the reference framework for the future organization and operationalization of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC).
The SSLG shall adopt the following Standing Committees: Executive Committee (EXECOM), Internal Affairs Committee, Secretariat Committee, Finance and Logistics Committee, Publicity and Technical Committee, and Learner Welfare and
Policy Management Committee. This structure shall serve as the standard organizational system of the SSLG.
All officers are expected to actively perform their assigned roles and responsibilities, including managing membership, ensuring participation, assigning tasks, and maintaining coordination, discipline, and accountability. All committees shall be organized and fully operational upon designation. Failure to comply shall be addressed in accordance with existing learner government policies.
All committees shall proceed with their organization and begin performing their respective functions upon designation. The SSLG shall ensure that all committees are properly constituted and operational.
Enclosed herewith are the following:
Annex A: Supreme Secondary Learner Government S.Y. 2026-2027 Standing Committees and its Composition; and
Annex B: Guidelines for the Learner Government Coordinating Council Establishment of Standing Committees and their Composition for S.Y. 2026-2027.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. SOPHIA ELISE C. AGUAS, Public Information Officer/Spokesperson at sophiaagua0223@gmail.com or via Facebook (sophia.aguas.273184).
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
As an alternative, the Chairperson may open committee positions to interested Representatives, subject to a screening process to ensure suitability and equitable participation.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
The final composition of each committee shall be presented to and approved by the body through a formal motion or resolution during a duly convened meeting of the SSLG.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that event setups are visually organized, presentable, and aligned with the theme.
Coordinate with the Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine appropriate actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.

===== OM_SSLG_2026_002 (1).docx =====
MEMORANDUM
No. 002, s. 2026
FOR:ALL CLUB AND ORGANIZATION PRESIDENTS
ALL CLUB AND ORGANIZATION ADVISERS
SUPREME SECONDARY LEARNER GOVERNMENT (SSLG) OFFICERS
ALL OTHERS CONCERNED
FROM: Hon. RHIAN JADE L. CASTRO
Chairperson, Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) President Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG)
SUBJECT:
IMPLEMENTATION OF PROJECT ECOLEAD: THE LGCC SCHOOL CLEAN-UP AND ENVIRONMENTAL STEWARDSHIP PROGRAM FOR SCHOOL YEAR 2026–2027
DATE: July 28, 2026
Pursuant to the commitment of the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC) to promote environmental responsibility, discipline, and shared accountability among learner organizations, this Office hereby announces the implementation of Project EcoLead, the LGCC School Clean-Up and Environmental Stewardship Program for School Year 2026–2027. The program serves as the Council’s flagship environmental initiative, empowering student leaders and learner organizations to take an active role in maintaining a clean, safe, and sustainable school environment.
Project EcoLead shall be implemented beginning July 31, 2026. Thereafter, participating clubs and organizations shall conduct their clean-up activities twice every month, preferably during the Second and Fourth weeks, on a schedule agreed upon by the organization and its assigned SSLG Monitoring Officer, in accordance with the approved Area Rotation Schedule.
Participation in the program shall be mandatory for all recognized clubs and organizations under the Learner Government Coordinating Council (LGCC). Each organization shall perform clean-up activities in its designated area under the supervision of its respective adviser.
To ensure equitable responsibility and accountability, assigned areas shall rotate monthly among participating organizations. The official Area Rotation Schedule is attached as Annex A.
Each club or organization shall be assigned an SSLG Officer who shall oversee the implementation of the activity, provide guidance, assess the performance of the assigned organization, and submit the necessary monitoring reports. The SSLG Monitoring Officer Assignment is attached as Annex B.
All participating organizations shall observe proper waste segregation, maintain discipline throughout the activity, utilize cleaning materials responsibly, and ensure that their assigned area is left clean and orderly after every scheduled clean-up.
For clarifications, please contact Hon. AARON Y. HERNANDEZ – Vice President/Spokesperson at aaronher1030@gmail.com or www.facebook.com/aaron.hernandez.914981.
For strict compliance.

ANNEX B:
OM-SSLG-2026-001 - GUIDELINES FOR THE LEARNER GOVERNMENT COORDINATING COUNCIL ESTABLISHMENT OF STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR COMPOSITION FOR S.Y. 2026-2027
GENERAL PROVISIONS
All Officers of the Supreme Secondary Learner Government (SSLG) are hereby directed to establish functional Standing Committees within the school to ensure efficient, transparent, and responsive student governance.
The establishment and composition of these committees shall be aligned with the provisions of the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws, and anchored on the principles of participatory leadership, accountability, and service-oriented governance within the school community.
The SSLG shall establish the following school-based standing committees: a. Executive Committee; b. Internal Affairs Committee; c. Secretariat Committee; d. Finance and Logistics Committee; e. Publicity and Technical Committee; and f. Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee.
The SSLG President shall issue a local school memorandum or resolution to guide the student body, class officers, and SSLG advisers.
The SSLG Vice President shall oversee the organization of these school-based committees, while ensuring an equitable distribution of responsibilities among all elected SSLG officers and grade-level representatives.
COMPOSITION OF COMMITTEES
Each Standing Committee shall have the following minimum composition:
The Executive Committee shall compose of the President as the Chairperson, Vice President as the Co-Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are members.
The Internal Affairs Committee shall compose of the Vice President as the Chairperson, Secretary as the Secretary General, and the rest of the officers are Members.
The Secretariat Committee shall compose of the Secretary as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Finance and Logistics Committee shall consist of the Treasurer as the Chairperson, Auditor as the Co-Chair, and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Publicity and Technical Committee shall consist of the Public Information Officer as the Chairperson, one (1) Co-Chair, and 1-3 Members (from the Representatives).
The Learner Welfare and Policy Management Committee shall consist of the Protocol Officer as Chairperson and 1-2 Members (from the Representatives).
The Chairperson of each committee shall designate or appoint the members from among the Representatives of the SSLG, based on competence, availability, and relevance of function.
No Officer shall serve as Chairperson, Co-Chairperson, Secretariat, or Member in more than one (1) committee, except when deemed necessary by the body to ensure functionality and continuity of operations.
SPECIFIC STANDING COMMITTEES AND THEIR FUNCTIONS
EXECUTIVE COMMITTEE (EXECOM)
Make final decisions on proposed programs, projects, and activities.
Review and approve plans submitted by committees.
Set priorities and overall direction of the SSLG.
Resolve issues elevated by committees.
Ensure alignment of all committees with SSLG goals.
INTERNAL AFFAIRS COMMITTEE
Monitor progress of committee tasks and deliverables.
Follow up pending outputs and deadlines.
Track participation and involvement of officers.
Provide performance updates to leadership.
Address internal coordination concerns.
SECRETARIAT COMMITTEE
Prepare meeting agendas in coordination with leadership.
Record minutes and document discussions.
Compile and organize reports and official documents.
Handle official communications (letters, notices, endorsements).
Maintain organized records and filing systems.
FINANCE AND LOGISTICS COMMITTEE
Prepare and manage budget proposals.
Monitor expenses and ensure proper fund utilization.
Handle financial documentation and reporting.
Procure materials and resources needed for activities.
Ensure readiness of logistical requirements (venue, equipment, supplies).
Assist in physical setup and arrangement of materials during activities.
PUBLICITY AND TECHNICAL COMMITTEE
Design and release publication materials (posters, announcements, captions).
Manage official social media and information dissemination.
Document activities through photos, videos, and write-ups.
Handle technical setup (sound system, presentations, ICT needs).
Conceptualize and assist in venue design, decoration, and overall visual setup of activities.
Ensure that events are visually organized and aligned with the theme.
Partner with Finance and Logistics Committee for materials and execution.
F. LEARNER WELFARE AND POLICY MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Receive and document student concerns, complaints, and grievances.
Evaluate concerns and determine suitable actions based on existing policies.
Provide policy and legal basis for decisions, actions, and recommendations, ensuring alignment with school rules, DepEd policies, and the SSLG Constitution and By-Laws.
Coordinate with school authorities regarding student-related issues.
Recommend appropriate interventions and resolutions.
Assist in implementing and disseminating learner-related policies.
Promote student welfare, discipline, inclusivity, and protection.
OPERATIONAL GUIDELINES
Committees shall conduct regular meetings and submit reports.
All actions and recommendations of committees shall be subject to deliberation and approval by the body.
Transparency, collaboration, and accountability shall be observed in all committee undertakings.
EFFECTIVITY
This Memorandum shall take effect immediately upon issuance and shall remain in force for S.Y. 2026–2027 unless otherwise amended or revoked.
Prepared by:
RHIAN JADE L. CASTROSSLG President
Reviewed and Evaluated by:
AARON KHRISTIAN T. BANAGSSLG Adviser
Concurred:
AMPARO M. MUÑOZ, EDDPrincipal IV
